### [1] [EditHero: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Part-Level 3D Editing and Vibe Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02298)
*Ruihan Yu,Yu-Ju Tsai,Muyao Niu,Runyi Li,Lian Fu,Hanqing Liu,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yonghao Yu,Sho Kuno,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EditHero，首个面向长程、部件级3D编辑的基准，包含自然语言指令和几何/纹理目标图像，并用它比较非智能体与LLM/VLM智能体两类3D编辑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法通常仅测试单次编辑，但真实资产是经过一长串修订构建的，每次编辑都需实现目标改动而保持其他部分不变，缺乏相应基准来评估这种长程迭代编辑能力。

Method: 构建EditHero基准：一个确定性装配引擎保证每次编辑后精确生成目标结果，每条编辑序列均经人工审查；提供自然语言指令及几何和纹理目标图像。用该基准对比两类方法：非智能体方法自上而下，从学习的3D表示重新生成物体并推断需保留的内容；LLM/VLM智能体自下而上，通过检查网格并只重写指令要求部分的代码进行编辑。

Result: 非智能体方法常遗漏请求的改动并破坏应保持不变的区域；大多数LLM更严格遵循指令，且所有智能体方法在保留未编辑部分上表现更好，但每次编辑耗时数分钟。

Conclusion: EditHero为可靠迭代3D编辑研究提供了基准，作者将发布装配引擎和编辑序列以支持后续研究。

Abstract: 3D editing methods are usually tested on a single edit, yet an asset is built through a long sequence of revisions, each of which must implement the requested change while leaving everything else unchanged. We introduce EditHero, to our knowledge the first benchmark for long-horizon, part-level 3D editing, with natural-language instructions and target images for both geometry and texture. A deterministic assembly engine produces the exact target after every edit, and every sequence is reviewed by hand. We use EditHero to compare 2 opposite approaches to 3D editing. Non-agentic methods operate top down, regenerating the object from a learned 3D representation and inferring what to keep. In contrast, LLM/VLM agents operate bottom up, editing through code that inspects the mesh and rewrites only the parts required by instructions. The non-agentic methods often miss the requested change and disturb regions that should stay fixed. Most LLMs follow instructions more closely, and all of them preserve the unedited parts better, but each of their edits takes minutes. We will release the engine and the edit sequences to support research on reliable iterative 3D editing.

</details>


### [2] [DeskForge: Dense Supervision from Desktop Environments for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02320)
*A. Said Gurbuz,Ahmed Nassar,Sunghwan Hong,Marc Pollefeys,Peter W. J. Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可控桌面环境 DeskForge，合成百万级桌面标注数据，微调视觉语言模型后显著提升 GUI 定位与长程任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体需在复杂桌面场景中可靠定位操作目标，但多应用、窗口重叠与视觉相似控件造成干扰；现有训练数据缺少此类场景的密集标注，也缺乏可控的变化方式。

Method: 构建可控桌面环境 DeskForge，组合并探索真实应用，变化应用状态、内容、窗口布局、外观与分辨率，融合截图、无障碍树与窗口几何信息生成密集元素标注，并记录动作执行结果；据此构建含 120 万条标注观测、1.597 亿元素实例的 DeskForge-1M，并用其中 20 万条定位样本微调四个视觉语言模型。

Result: 四个模型在留出桌面条件和五个外部 GUI 定位基准上均提升；Qwen3.5-4B 在 ScreenSpot-Pro 提升 11.51 个百分点，在 OSWorld-G 提升 10.11 个百分点。固定规划器下，长程任务完成数增加：WebArena-Infinity 由 31/119 升至 50/119，OpenApps 由 3/100 升至 15/100。

Conclusion: 可控地组合真实桌面环境可为 GUI 定位与长程计算机使用提供可扩展的监督来源；框架代码、数据集与微调模型已在项目主页开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents need to reliably ground action targets in complex desktop scenes, where multiple applications, overlapping windows, and visually similar controls compete for attention. Existing training data rarely pair such scenes with dense annotations or vary them in a controlled way. We introduce DeskForge, a controllable desktop environment that composes and explores real applications to generate large-scale supervision for computer-use agents. It varies application states, content, window layout, appearance, and resolution, and fuses screenshots, accessibility trees, and window geometry into dense element annotations while recording the outcome of each executed action. Using this environment, we construct DeskForge-1M, a corpus of 1.2M annotated desktop observations containing 159.7M element instances. We fine-tune four vision-language models on 200K grounding examples drawn from DeskForge-1M. All four improve across held-out desktop conditions and on all five external GUI grounding benchmarks; for Qwen3.5-4B, accuracy increases by 11.51 percentage points on ScreenSpot-Pro and 10.11 points on OSWorld-G. The gains also translate to long-horizon task completion: under a fixed planner, the fine-tuned action models solve more WebArena-Infinity and OpenApps tasks, with Qwen3.5-4B increasing from 31 to 50 of 119 tasks and from 3 to 15 of 100 tasks, respectively. These results show that controllable composition of real desktop environments provides a scalable source of supervision for improving both GUI grounding and long-horizon computer use. The framework code, the dataset, and the fine-tuned model are available from the project page: https://saidgurbuz.github.io/deskforge/

</details>


### [3] [SCION: Scene Composition with Instanced Neural Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02322)
*William Koch,Amogh Joshi,Cyrus Vachha,Cheng Zheng,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCION提出一种层次化组合式场景表示，用可复用的基本图元词汇表和世界空间实例替换独立的3D高斯，实现紧凑、可控的高质量神经场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景具有组合性，砖块、草叶、石子、树叶等元素会重复出现。然而现有神经场景表示（如3D Gaussian Splatting及后续的抽象与压缩方法）将每个元素视为独特个体，为每个场景拟合数百万个独立高斯，导致参数冗余、操纵能力弱。此前的方法如Splat and Replace虽然拟合模板对象，但需要大量人工选择重复元素。

Method: SCION是一种层次化组合场景表示，用一组紧凑的可复用图元词汇表加上轻量级世界空间实例来替换独立高斯，将变换后的副本放置到场景各处。通过对离散和连续场景参数进行联合优化，将表示拟合到多视图捕获，结合splat和实例两级致密化，以及保持共享图元细节的对抗损失。

Result: 恢复出的结构在保持高质量的同时实现了紧凑、可控的表示，即使在1.2 MB下仍能维持高质量。SCION取得了优于现有高斯压缩方法的率失真性能，并支持无需重新训练的实例级场景编辑与动画。

Conclusion: 结果表明神经场景表示无需将场景记忆为独立图元，而是可以发现可复用的部件。项目网页：https://light.princeton.edu/SCION

Abstract: Real-world scenes are compositional: bricks, blades of grass, pebbles, and tree leaves recur across human-built and natural environments. Existing neural scene representations model these elements independently. Most 3D Gaussian Splatting and follow-up abstraction and compression methods treat each element as unique, fitting millions of independent Gaussians per scene. Prior methods like Splat and Replace fit template objects, but they require mostly manual selection of repeated elements. As a result, these representations store redundant parameters and provide weak manipulation handles for downstream tasks. We introduce SCION, a hier- archical compositional scene representation that replaces independent Gaussians with a compact vocabulary of reusable primitives and lightweight world-space instances that place transformed copies throughout the scene. We fit this represen- tation to multi-view captures via a joint optimization over discrete and continuous scene parameters, combining two-level densification over splats and instances with an adversarial loss that preserves detail across shared primitives. The recovered structure yields a compact, controllable representation while maintaining high quality even at 1.2 MB. SCION achieves rate-distortion favorable to existing Gaussian compression methods, and it enables instance-level scene editing and animation without retraining. Our results show that neural scene representations need not memorize scenes as independent primitives; they can discover reusable parts. Project webpage: https://light.princeton.edu/SCION

</details>


### [4] [SCOPE-4D: Endoscopic 4D Geometry Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02343)
*Chaoyi Zhou,Zhongpai Gao,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Run Wang,Terrence Chen,Siyu Huang,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE-4D是一个内窥镜4D几何基础模型，单次前向即可从单目RGB视频联合预测相机参数、稠密几何与3D组织轨迹，并配套构建了约5,000段视频的SCOPE-5K数据集与CRM运动约束，在多项基准上取得优越的几何估计、3D跟踪与长序列重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜导航与机器人辅助需要几何理解，但学习可靠的内窥镜几何面临两大挑战：几何标注稀缺，以及相机运动与组织形变之间的歧义。该工作旨在通过大规模监督与运动约束解决这两大问题。

Method: 提出SCOPE-4D内窥镜4D几何基础模型，单次前向从单目RGB视频联合预测相机参数、稠密几何和3D组织轨迹。通过数据整理与标注流程构建SCOPE-5K数据集，含约5,000段真实与合成胃肠道内窥镜及腹腔镜片段，提供丰富几何监督并新增体模与真实结肠镜评测集。方法包括在SCOPE-5K上进行几何监督微调以学习内窥镜先验，并引入Common–Residual Motion（CRM）约束相对公共组织运动的局部形变，结合轨迹监督。

Result: 在公开基准与新建基准上评估，展现强域内与域外几何能力、更优的3D跟踪性能以及更稳定的长序列结肠重建；盲法用户研究支持其在真实临床视频上的重建质量感知。CRM与轨迹监督结合几何监督，相比仅几何微调进一步提升了相机与深度估计，并实现稠密3D组织跟踪。

Conclusion: 结果表明，大规模内窥镜监督与运动约束对联合几何估计与组织跟踪具有重要价值。

Abstract: Geometric understanding supports endoscopic navigation and robotic assistance, but learning reliable endoscopic geometry faces two challenges: scarce geometric annotations and ambiguity between camera motion and tissue deformation. We present SCOPE-4D, an endoscopic 4D geometry foundation model that jointly predicts camera parameters, dense geometry, and 3D tissue trajectories from monocular RGB video in a single forward pass. Our curation and annotation pipeline constructs SCOPE-5K, a collection of approximately 5,000 clips spanning real and synthetic gastrointestinal endoscopy and laparoscopy. The collection provides rich geometric supervision and includes newly collected phantom and real-colonoscopy evaluation sets. Geometric supervised fine-tuning on SCOPE-5K learns endoscopic priors that improve camera and depth estimation. Common--Residual Motion (CRM) further constrains local deformation relative to common tissue movement. Together with geometric supervision, CRM and trajectory supervision further improve camera and depth estimation over geometric fine-tuning alone while enabling dense 3D tissue tracking. Evaluations on public and newly collected benchmarks demonstrate strong in-domain and out-of-domain geometry, superior 3D tracking, and more stable long-sequence colon reconstruction. A blinded user study further supports the perceived reconstruction quality on real clinical video. Together, these results demonstrate the value of large-scale endoscopic supervision and motion constraints for joint geometry estimation and tissue tracking.

</details>


### [5] [Confidence-Controlled XAI Auditing for Pedestrian Detection under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02364)
*Ruben Dario Florez-Zela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了驾驶域偏移下行人检测器后置解释的忠实性，发现基于删除的忠实度与检测强度强烈耦合，控制检测强度后D-RISE忠实度仍具域依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车辆感知模型日益需要可解释性，但解释在驾驶域偏移下是否仍保持忠实尚不明确，尤其缺乏对检测强度混淆因素的控制。

Method: 对固定的YOLOv8s行人检测器在PIE和JAAD数据集上进行后置解释审计，采用基于ROI的D-Deletion、冻结置信度三分位、基于秩的检验、自助法区间以及Holm校正；并加入非扰动式EigenCAM基线进行比较。

Result: 删除式忠实度与解释时的检测强度强耦合，D-RISE的Spearman相关系数在0.70至0.82之间，使朴素置信度分层比较不可靠；在固定f0分箱内控制检测强度后，D-RISE忠实度在中心f0区间仍具域依赖性，PIE的D-Deletion高于JAAD且经Holm校正后显著；EigenCAM基线忠实度低于D-RISE，但也表现出分数耦合。

Conclusion: 基于删除的评估设置本身可能导致解释忠实度与检测强度耦合，结果并非D-RISE特有，应推动在域偏移下对安全关键感知开展置信度受控的XAI审计。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly required for perception models in intelligent vehicles, yet whether explanations remain faithful under driving domain shift is still poorly understood. This work audits post-hoc explanations of a fixed YOLOv8s pedestrian detector across PIE and JAAD using ROI-based D-Deletion, frozen confidence terciles, rank-based tests, bootstrap intervals, and Holm correction. The audit shows that deletion-based faithfulness is strongly coupled to detection strength at explanation time, with Spearman correlations between 0.70 and 0.82 for D-RISE, making naive confidence-stratified comparisons unreliable. After controlling for detection strength within fixed f0 bins, D-RISE faithfulness remains domain-dependent in the central f0 range, with PIE showing higher D-Deletion than JAAD and Holm-adjusted significance. A non-perturbative EigenCAM baseline is less faithful than D-RISE but also exhibits score coupling, suggesting that the effect is not specific to D-RISE and is related to the deletion-based evaluation setup. These results motivate confidence-controlled XAI audits for safety-critical perception under domain shift.

</details>


### [6] [EviDent-CBCT: Evidence-Bottlenecked Report Generation from Dental CBCT under Non-Exhaustive Report Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02375)
*Ruiyang Hao,Zhi Qin Tan,Yulan He,Owen Addison,Yunpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EviDent-CBCT 框架，用"证据瓶颈"结构从牙齿 CBCT 影像生成结构化报告，在弱监督下取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙颌面 CBCT 单次扫描可产生数十项牙齿、解剖和空间层面的发现，但从有限临床数据学习报告生成很困难：常规报告未必完整记录影像发现，"未提及"可能代表不存在，也可能代表未报告，属于不完整监督问题。

Method: 解剖感知网络将每例 CBCT 映射为牙齿级、全局及 tooth-IAC 的离散证据记录；牙科逻辑一致性投影在生成前调和不相容证据；确定性渲染器加图像盲局部语言模型仅依据该记录生成报告。牙齿级证据采用可靠性感知训练，把合格的非提及用作降权负样本，而未报告的全局与 tooth-IAC 标签保持未知。另设金属敏感输入通道保留牙科材料的强度线索。

Result: 三次验证运行中，合并证据集 F1 为 0.666±0.006，RadFact-Lite-Dental 逻辑 F1 为 0.402±0.003，而最强可控直接基线仅为 0.371±0.018。在 ODIN 2026 挑战赛隐藏测试集上，自动评估排名第二，盲法临床 Arena 对比排名第三。

Conclusion: 结果表明离散证据记录是 CBCT 报告生成的一种有效且可审计的接口。

Abstract: Dento-maxillofacial cone-beam CT (CBCT) reports may contain dozens of tooth-specific, anatomical, and spatial findings from a single 3D scan. Learning to generate such reports from limited clinical data is challenging because routine reports may not exhaustively document image findings, and a non-mention may reflect either absence or non-reporting. We present EviDent-CBCT, an evidence-bottlenecked framework designed for this incomplete supervision. An anatomy-aware network maps each CBCT scan to a discrete record of tooth-level, global, and tooth-IAC evidence. A dental-logic consistency projection reconciles incompatible evidence before a deterministic renderer and an image-blind local language model generate the report using only this record. For tooth-level evidence, reliability-aware training uses eligible non-mentions as reduced-weight negatives, while unreported global and tooth-IAC labels remain unknown. A metal-sensitive input channel preserves intensity cues from dental materials. Across three validation runs, EviDent-CBCT achieves $0.666\pm0.006$ merged evidence set-F1 and $0.402\pm0.003$ RadFact-Lite-Dental logical-F1, versus $0.371\pm0.018$ for the strongest controlled direct baseline. In the ODIN 2026 challenge, it ranked second in automated evaluation and third in blinded clinical Arena comparison on the hidden test set. These results support the discrete evidence record as an effective and auditable interface for CBCT report generation.

</details>


### [7] [FactorSplat: Appearance-Controllable Gaussian Proxies for Medical Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02382)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FactorSplat，一种按场景训练的N维高斯泼溅代理，支持推理时按区域更改强度到RGBA的曲线，实现可交互编辑的医学体渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学体渲染中传输函数（TF）控制颜色与可见性，但以往基于图像训练的高斯代理通常将单一TF"烘焙"进外观，无法在推理时更改TF，限制了结构隐藏/显示等交互编辑能力。

Method: 采用每场景的N维高斯泼溅（N-DGS）代理：本地查找应用作者指定的颜色与不透明度变化，共享功能编码器加低秩逐高斯因子学习残差外观响应；几何与方向外观在各预设间共享，并通过可见性控制与TF感知剪枝保留隐藏/显示结构的能力。

Result: 在7例CT与MR扫描上，于验证、插值、未见组合和OOD编辑四种划分中均优于region-aware VEG，平均PSNR增益1.10–1.52 dB，改変区域误差更低；每扫描仅需一个检查点即可支持未见编辑；1600²分辨率下缓存快速渲染器平均524 FPS，TF切换1.17 ms。

Conclusion: FactorSplat实现了推理时区域特定TF编辑的医学体渲染代理，无需重训练即可支持未见编辑，并保持高分辨率下的实时性能。

Abstract: Transfer functions (TFs) control color and visibility in medical volume rendering, but image-trained Gaussian proxies typically bake one transfer function into their appearance. We present FactorSplat, a per-scene N-dimensional Gaussian splatting (N-DGS) proxy that accepts region-specific intensity-to-RGBA curves at inference. A local lookup applies the authored color and opacity change, while a shared functional encoder and low-rank per-Gaussian factors learn the residual appearance response. Geometry and directional appearance remain shared across presets, with visibility control and TF-aware pruning preserving the ability to hide and reveal structures. On seven CT and MR scans, FactorSplat improves mean PSNR and changed-region error over region-aware VEG across validation, interpolation, unseen composition, and out-of-distribution (OOD) edits. Across these four splits, seven-scan mean PSNR gains over VEG range from 1.10 to 1.52 dB. One checkpoint per scan supports unseen edits without retraining. At $1600^2$, the cached fast renderer averages 524 FPS with 1.17 ms TF switches. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/FactorSplat/.

</details>


### [8] [Octrees as an Explicit 3D Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02388)
*Ran Dan,Si-Tong Wei,Pengfei Xiong,Wei Zhang,Yadong Mu,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctLLM 用显式稀疏八叉树 token 表示几何，并通过独立可训练分支为冻结的视觉-语言主干注入 3D 能力，在统一多模态 LLM 中取得新 SOTA 且不损失通用语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 大语言模型存在两大缺陷：一是将形状压缩为隐式码本索引或坐标文本，使模型观测中丢失了空间结构；二是通过微调主干来获得 3D 模态能力，从而覆盖了其通用语言能力。作者希望同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 几何以显式的八叉树占据 token 序列输入；为控制序列长度，随机清空倒数第二层节点并省略其后代而保持形状，得到更短、坐标与深度锚定的稀疏八叉树（S-Octree），用于位置感知的掩码建模生成与 3D 理解。在参数层面，不走全量微调或 LoRA，而是在与预训练参数分离的部分中新增 3D 容量：mesh token 经过少数模块中独立可训练分支，文本与图像 token 保留冻结的视觉-语言通路，两路通过共享自注意力交互。

Result: 训练参数远少于全量微调，却在统一多模态 LLM 中取得新 SOTA：相比 ShapeLLM-Omni，图像到 3D 的 FID 降低 17.4%，渲染接地的图像描述指标提升 28.7 个点，同时在通用语言基准上与主干持平。

Conclusion: 显式稀疏八叉树序列表示与独立可训练 3D 分支的结合，可在保留通用语言能力的同时高效注入 3D 空间理解与生成能力。

Abstract: Existing 3D large language models (LLMs) compromise on two fronts: they compress shapes into latent codebook indices or coordinate text, which removes spatial structure from what the model observes, and they acquire the 3D modality by fine-tuning the backbone, which overwrites its general language ability. We present OctLLM, which addresses both limitations. Geometry enters as an explicit 3D sequence of octree occupancy tokens. However, full octree sequences grow rapidly with depth; OctLLM therefore randomly empties penultimate-level nodes and omits descendants while preserving shape, yielding a shorter coordinate- and depth-anchored Sparse Octree (S-Octree) for position-aware mask-modeling generation and 3D understanding. On the other front, existing methods introduce a new modality with full fine-tuning or LoRA, but full fine-tuning is costly, LoRA limits 3D capacity, and both modify the language pathway. OctLLM instead adds 3D capacity in parameters separate from the pretrained ones: mesh tokens are routed through independent trainable branches in a subset of blocks while text and image tokens retain the frozen vision-language pathway, and the two streams interact through shared self-attention. It trains far fewer parameters than full fine-tuning, yet sets a new state of the art among unified multimodal LLMs, lowering image-to-3D FID by $17.4\%$ and raising render-grounded captioning by $28.7$ points over ShapeLLM-Omni, while matching the backbone on general language benchmarks.

</details>


### [9] [An AI-Based Multi-Stage Approach for Androgenetic Alopecia Assessment from Low-Magnification Scalp Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02421)
*Mahmoud Raslan,Nada Omar,Omar Khaled,Tarek Waleed,Mohamed Hazem,Rania Mounir,Solwan Elsamanoudy,Ahmed Mourad,Noura Adel,Muhammad Rushdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个自动化头皮分析与临床决策支持框架，结合毛囊单位定位、毛发数量计数、毛发直径估计、区域聚合和可解释规则层，用于雄激素性脱发（AGA）的定量评估，并验证了其检测与分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGA以毛囊微型化、单毛发毛囊单位增多和毛发直径改变为特征，临床上需要客观、可量化的评估手段，而人工毛发镜判读主观性强、效率低，因此作者希望构建辅助而非替代皮肤科医生解读的自动化定量分析系统。

Method: 研究构建了一个自动化头皮分析与临床决策支持框架，包括毛囊单位定位、序数式可见毛发计数、标定后的毛干宽度估计、区域聚合以及可解释规则层。临床队列纳入243例患者（127例AGA、116例非AGA）；计算机视觉实验使用160例专家标注患者、2400张毛发镜图像和约15.8万条毛囊单位标注。评估中采用患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）划分以检验泛化性。

Result: 在患者不重叠评估下，YOLOv8m取得测试mAP@0.5=0.920、召回率=0.860；带support-map通道的EfficientNet-B5达到87.0%的专家框计数准确率（macro F1=0.85）。另用500张图像以检测器生成框进行端到端处理，相对于人类专家标注，毛囊检测MAE为6.56、毛囊分类MAE为16.59。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现毛囊定位、计数与宽度估计的自动化定量分析，并提供可解释的规则化输出，性能接近专家标注水平；其定位为辅助皮肤科医生判读的决策支持工具，而非替代医生诊断。

Abstract: Androgenetic alopecia (AGA) is characterized by patterned follicular miniaturization, increased single-hair follicular units, and altered hair-shaft diameter. We present an automated quantitative scalp-analysis and clinical decision-support framework combining FU localization, ordinal visible-shaft counting, calibrated shaft-width estimation, regional aggregation, and an interpretable rule layer. The clinical cohort comprised 243 patients (127 AGA, 116 non-AGA), while the computer-vision experiments used 160 expert-annotated patients, 2,400 trichoscopic images, and approximately 158,000 FU annotations. Under patientdisjoint evaluation, YOLOv8m achieved test mAP@0.5=0.920 and recall=0.860; EfficientNet-B5 with a support-map channel achieved 87.0% expert-box count accuracy (macro F1=0.85). A separate 500-image set was processed end-to-end with detector-generated boxes, yielding MAE of 6.56 for follicle detection and 16.59 for follicle classification relative to human-expert annotations. The system is intended to assist, rather than replace, dermatologist interpretation.

</details>


### [10] [A Simulation-Grounded Agentic VLM Framework for Wildfire Monitoring and Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02451)
*Duowen Chen,Yuchen Sun,Zhiqi Li,Yuxuan Liao,Sinan Wang,Bart van Bloemen Waanders,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个以仿真为基础的视觉语言模型（VLM）框架，将2D野火仿真自动转换为带标注的视频片段，并构建免训练的多智能体VLM系统用于野火监测与结构化报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的野火监测需要将视觉证据与物理火灾动态关联，但同步物理标注的真实视频稀缺，且高保真3D仿真成本高昂。因此需要一种低成本、可复用标注的替代方案。

Method: 用固定Blender映射把2D野火仿真转成低细节3D代理，对齐地形、燃料布局、火情与风场线索；再通过可控视频生成增强外观。生成的视频与仿真标签构成可复用的多模态记忆，供免训练多智能体VLM系统检索参考片段、融合视觉与记忆预测，并输出结构化野火报告。

Result: 在留出的生成片段上，视频记忆达到51.5%的四标签精确准确率，直接VLM查询为22.6%，纯文本记忆为16–17%；完整系统在六个仿真衍生报告字段上达到77.3%准确率。并通过组件消融、跨生成器测试和三项真实无人机评估验证了检索、报告、生成器变化及可观测监测任务。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实物理标注稀缺的条件下，将自动仿真到代理的转换与基于记忆的VLM推理相连接，为野火监测提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: Effective wildfire monitoring requires relating visual evidence to physical fire dynamics, yet real videos with synchronized physical annotations are scarce and high-fidelity 3D simulation is costly. We present a simulation-grounded vision-language model (VLM) framework that automatically converts 2D wildfire simulations into labeled video episodes. A fixed Blender mapping produces low-detail 3D proxies aligned with simulator terrain, fuel layout, fire activity, and wind cues; controllable video generation supplies richer appearance. The proxies are intermediate representations rather than finely rendered final scenes. Generated videos and simulator labels form reusable multimodal memory for a training-free multi-agent VLM system that retrieves reference episodes, reconciles visual and memory-based predictions, and produces structured wildfire reports. On held-out generated episodes, video memory achieves 51.5% exact four-tag accuracy, compared with 22.6% for direct VLM querying and 16-17% for text-only memory; the complete system achieves 77.3% accuracy on six simulator-derived report fields. Component ablations, cross-generator tests, and three real-UAV evaluations assess retrieval, reporting, generator changes, and observable monitoring tasks. The framework connects automatic simulation-to-proxy conversion with memory-based VLM reasoning under scarce real-world physical annotations.

</details>


### [11] [DeepStratNet: A Context-Aware Coordinate Regression Framework for Seismic Horizon Tracking under Sparse Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02494)
*Aniq Ahmad,Musham Ahmad Malik,Ahmad Mustafa,Heather Bedle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将地层自动追踪从稠密语义分割重构为有界坐标回归问题，用轻量回归头加LSTM建模，配合地质连续性正则，在定量、定性及稀疏鲁棒性上均优于分割方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动层位追踪是3D地震解释的基础任务，但现有深度学习方法多将其建模为稠密语义分割。这种范式存在多重缺陷：需要后处理从概率图中提取精确层位坐标，同时训练时又要把时间/深度拾取转换为稠密掩码；未拾取的地震道被当作背景，可能损害收敛；前后处理都会引入误差。此外，2D分割模型无法捕捉层间上下文，而3D模型计算代价过高。

Method: 作者将层位追踪建模为有界坐标回归问题，令模型在每个横向位置直接预测目标层位的时间/深度坐标。提出一个轻量回归头，可与任意预训练视觉骨干兼容，并耦合LSTM模块建模层间（切片间）上下文，从而在整个数据体上生成连续层位曲面。损失方面，用L1与L2损失组合监督有效层位拾取处的预测，并引入地质信息启发的正则项以约束相邻地震道之间的横向连续性。

Result: 在受控实验条件下，作者在新西兰某地震数据体上，对四种预训练视觉骨干分别在分割与回归两种配置下进行评估。所提方法在RMSE、PCC等指标上定量一致优于对应的分割方案，定性上同样更优，并且对训练拾取稀疏度的增加表现出更强的鲁棒性。此外，预测沿相邻地震道的变化能够捕捉局部地质复杂度差异，可作为下游地震解释的自动化质量控制手段。

Conclusion: 层位追踪可更自然地表述为坐标回归而非稠密分割，回归式框架配合层间上下文建模与地质连续性正则，在精度、鲁棒性和自动化质量控制方面均带来收益。

Abstract: Automatic horizon tracking is a foundational task in 3D seismic interpretation. Most existing deep learning approaches formulate it as dense semantic segmentation, typically using U-Net-based architectures. The model produces a probability map over all pixels that must be post-processed to extract precise horizon coordinates, while horizon picks in time/depth must be converted into dense masks for training. Unpicked seismic traces are consequently treated as background, which can hinder convergence, and both pre- and post-processing can introduce errors into the final interpretation. Moreover, 2D segmentation models do not inherently capture inter-slice context, while 3D models are often computationally prohibitive. We instead formulate horizon tracking as a bounded coordinate regression problem, where the model directly predicts the time/depth coordinate of the target horizon at each lateral position. We propose a lightweight regression head compatible with any pretrained vision backbone, coupled with an LSTM module to model inter-slice context and produce a continuous horizon surface across the volume. A combination of L1 and L2 losses supervises predictions at valid horizon picks, while a geology-informed regularization enforces lateral continuity between successive traces. Under controlled experimental conditions, we evaluate four pretrained vision backbones under both segmentation and regression configurations on a seismic volume from New Zealand. The proposed approach consistently outperforms its segmentation counterparts quantitatively, using metrics including RMSE and PCC, and qualitatively, while also demonstrating greater robustness to increasing sparsity of training picks. Finally, we show that prediction variation across successive traces captures local variations in geological complexity, providing an automated quality control measure for downstream seismic interpretation.

</details>


### [12] [MeshQuery: Agentic Seam Planning for UV Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02507)
*Marco Schouten,Arthur Roullier,Elie Michel,Ruben Wiersma,Axel Paris,Tamy Boubekeur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshQuery 是一种免训练的智能体式自动 UV 展开方法，用 VLM 规划艺术家风格的接缝，通过可查询网格表示与 DSL 表达接缝程序，并结合反馈迭代优化，在质量指标和艺术家偏好上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级四边形网格的自动 UV 展开需要符合艺术家习惯的接缝规划，传统方法难以兼顾高层意图与底层边选择，且自回归预测方式难以扩展到大网格。作者希望将高层意图规划与底层边选择解耦。

Method: 提出 MeshQuery：由 VLM 在自然语言表达的 UV 展开领域知识条件下，使用一组边选择工具规划接缝，并借助反馈闭环迭代改进；设计了可查询的网格表示与领域特定语言（DSL），让智能体按需检索网格信息，将接缝方案表示为拓扑、几何与语义属性上的紧凑边选择算子程序，并根据 UV 质量反馈迭代优化。

Result: 在 Adobe Substance 3D 与 Toys4K 网格上，MeshQuery 相比最强基线生成的图表数少 2.9x/4.29x、接缝长度短 1.63x/1.7x，专业艺术家在 80.9% 的对比中更偏好其结果。

Conclusion: 将高层意图规划与底层边选择及紧凑网格表示解耦，使 MeshQuery 可运行于不同后端 VLM，并能扩展到比自回归接缝预测大一个数量级的网格。

Abstract: We present MeshQuery, a training-free agentic approach to automatic UV unwrapping of production-grade quad meshes. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) plans artist-aligned seams using a set of edge-selection tools, conditioned on domain-specific UV-unwrapping knowledge expressed in natural language and refined with a feedback loop. We design a queryable mesh representation together with a domain-specific language (DSL) that enables the agent to retrieve mesh information on demand, express a seam plan as a compact program of edge-selection operators over topological, geometric, and semantic mesh attributes, and iteratively refine it from UV quality feedback. On Adobe Substance 3D and Toys4K meshes, MeshQuery produces 2.9x/4.29x fewer charts and 1.63x/1.7x shorter seams than the strongest baseline, and professional artists prefer its results in 80.9% of comparisons. Ultimately, decoupling high-level intent planning from low-level edge selection and compact mesh representation lets MeshQuery run on different backend VLMs and scale to meshes an order of magnitude larger than autoregressive seam prediction

</details>


### [13] [From Fragments to Global Maps: Learning Vectorized Map Aggregation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02513)
*Ziwei Li,Yi-Tang Chen,Xiaoqi Wang,Wenbin He,Han-Wei Shen,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MapMergeLLM，将矢量化高精地图聚合表述为条件序列生成任务，用大语言模型直接预测聚合后的全局地图折线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知、定位与规划依赖大规模矢量化高精地图，而构建此类地图需将车辆轨迹中带噪声、碎片化、重叠的局部预测聚合为一致的全局地图。现有聚合方法多依赖手工规则进行片段关联与精炼，固定阈值难以应对道路结构与预测误差的变化，常需针对检测器调参或人工调整。

Method: 将矢量化地图聚合建模为条件序列生成：输入序列化的局部矢量化地图，由大语言模型直接输出聚合后的全局地图折线。为减少对特定上游检测器的依赖，使用从干净矢量地图经损坏（模拟代表性预测误差）生成的合成局部地图进行训练。引入具备几何感知预训练的坐标分词器以精确表示地图坐标，并提出线段级关联损失，显式监督同一地图元素在不同局部观测间的对应关系。

Result: 在Argoverse2和nuScenes数据集上，使用多种近期上游检测器进行实验，MapMergeLLM显著优于启发式与基于优化的聚合基线，且无需针对检测器重新训练。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的序列生成框架可替代依赖手工规则与固定阈值的传统聚合方法，在不针对特定检测器调参的情况下实现更优的矢量化地图聚合效果。

Abstract: Large-scale vectorized HD maps provide structured road information that is essential for perception, localization, and planning in autonomous driving. Constructing such maps requires aggregating noisy, fragmented, and overlapping local predictions collected along a vehicle trajectory into a coherent global map. Existing aggregation methods typically rely on hand-crafted rules for fragment association and refinement. However, a fixed set of thresholds cannot effectively handle variations in road structures and prediction errors, often requiring detector-specific tuning or manual adjustment. To address this limitation, we propose MapMergeLLM, a data-driven framework that formulates vectorized map aggregation as conditional sequence generation with a large language model. Given serialized local vectorized maps, our model directly predicts the aggregated global map polylines. To reduce dependence on any particular upstream detector, we train the model on synthetic local maps generated from clean vector maps using corruptions that simulate representative prediction errors. We further introduce a coordinate tokenizer with geometry-aware pretraining to precisely represent map coordinates. In addition, we propose a line-level association loss that explicitly supervises correspondences between local observations of the same map element. Experiments on Argoverse2 and nuScenes using multiple recent upstream detectors demonstrate that MapMergeLLM substantially outperforms heuristic and optimization-based aggregation baselines without detector-specific retraining.

</details>


### [14] [Spatial Memory Intelligence: Endowing World Models with Understanding-Driven Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02521)
*Ying Yang,Guiyu Zhang,Lianghua Huang,Chang Nie,Chenyang Si,Haofan Wang,Shaoshuai Shi,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SMI框架，首次系统性地利用理解模型（MLLM）进行长视频世界模型的空间记忆管理，通过四种协同原子操作提升记忆稀疏性、生成稳定性和空间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成与世界模型在交互娱乐和具身仿真中潜力巨大，但随着记忆序列增长、结构日益复杂，长程空间上下文的建模与管理变得愈发困难，亟需更智能、更系统的记忆管理策略。

Method: 基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）不断增强的空间推理能力与统一模型的愿景，提出SMI（Spatial Memory Intelligence）框架，系统性地将理解模型用于长视频世界模型的空间记忆管理，引入四种协同的原子操作：空间聚类、簇内稀疏化、动作感知检索和可靠性感知过滤。

Result: 在多种基线、基准测试和世界模型骨干网络上进行了大量实验，验证了SMI的有效性与泛化性，在记忆稀疏性、生成稳定性和空间一致性方面均取得全面提升。

Conclusion: SMI是首个系统利用理解模型管理长视频世界模型空间记忆的框架，证明了该思路的有效性与通用性，为长视频世界模型的记忆管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-video generation and world models have shown strong potential for interactive entertainment and embodied simulation by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions and historical memory. However, as memory sequences grow longer and their structures become increasingly complex, managing long-range spatial context becomes increasingly challenging, calling for a more intelligent and systematic memory-management strategy. Building on the advancing spatial reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and the broader vision of unified models, we propose Spatial Memory Intelligence (SMI), the first framework to systematically employ an understanding model for spatial-memory management in long-video world models. SMI introduces four coordinated atomic operations: spatial clustering, within-cluster sparsification, action-aware retrieval, and reliability-aware filtering. Extensive experiments across multiple baselines, benchmarks, and world-model backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of SMI, achieving comprehensive improvements in memory sparsity, generation stability, and spatial consistency.

</details>


### [15] [Oracle headroom without signal: null-calibrated evaluation of candidate selection for thermal heart rate estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02561)
*Mohammad Rakibur Rahman,Nhi Nguyen,Sasan Sharifipour,Le Nguyen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Miguel Bordallo López,Constantino Álvarez Casado*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出基于摄像头的生理监测中，使用“oracle”选择最接近参考的估计会因随机一致性而高估信号质量指数的潜力，并提出用顺序统计建模，强调oracle评估需报告候选数量、有效覆盖率和匹配的零对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号质量指数旨在无生理参考时选择可靠估计，但其潜力常用oracle（每窗口选最接近参考的估计）来评估。这种回顾性选择可能奖励偶然一致性，导致评估结果偏乐观，因此需要量化该效应。

Method: 用顺序统计建模：对K个与参考无关的独立候选，推导期望oracle误差近似按1/K下降。并在96段iBVP记录上分析热成像心率估计，每10秒窗口有168个候选。

Result: oracle平均绝对误差为0.91 bpm，而最佳固定配置为10.74 bpm，最佳质量指数为18.03 bpm，常量预测器为8.61 bpm。K=24时，来自另一段记录的前额信号与正确信号几乎无差别（4.62 vs 4.61 bpm）。

Conclusion: oracle评估会因随机选择而严重低估误差，其表现可远优于真实方法，因此oracle评估应报告候选数量、有效覆盖率和匹配的零对照，以避免夸大信号质量指数的实际价值。

Abstract: Camera-based physiological monitoring can produce multiple estimates from several facial regions, extraction methods, and processing settings. Signal quality indices aim to select reliable estimates without a physiological reference, and their potential is often assessed with an oracle that selects the estimate closest to the reference in each window. This retrospective selection can reward chance agreement. We model the effect with order statistics. For K independent candidates unrelated to the reference, the expected oracle error decreases approximately as 1/K. We analyze thermal heart rate estimation on 96 iBVP recordings with 168 candidates per 10 s window. The oracle achieves a mean absolute error of 0.91 bpm, compared with 10.74 bpm for the best fixed configuration, 18.03 bpm for the best quality index, and 8.61 bpm for a constant predictor. With K = 24, a forehead signal from another recording matches the correct one, with 4.62 against 4.61 bpm. Oracle evaluations should report candidate count, valid coverage, and matched null controls.

</details>


### [16] [DAGS: Disentangled Appearance-and-Geometry Steering of a Frozen Image DiT for Temporally Stabilized Generative Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02567)
*Karthik Mohan Kumar,Damian Andrysiak,Pedro Antonio Pena,Kunal Tyagi,Rama Harihara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAGS 提出一种轻量、无注意力、解耦外观与几何条件的方案，将小型卷积编码器特征以逐层逐元素残差注入冻结的图像 DiT，使单帧图像模型升级为可控、高质量的流式渲染器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 能生成高保真图像，但输出方差大，且对目标条件的忠实度高度依赖条件注入方式。通过注意力堆叠条件会带来二次成本，同时微调骨干网络有过拟合风险。作者希望在对齐外观与几何条件的同时保持冻结骨干的预训练先验，并实现独立可控的高质量渲染。

Method: DAGS 使用两个小型卷积编码器，每帧计算一次条件特征，并以学习到的、逐层、逐元素的残差注入图像 token，避免注意力的二次开销。控制与时间处理位于冻结骨干之外，从而保留预训练先验、消除骨干过拟合风险。此外加入小型循环光照稳定器与免训练的时间引导项，把逐帧图像模型提升为流式渲染器。

Result: 在匹配的 1-spp + G-buffer 输入下，单帧 DAGS 相比实时降噪器 Intel OIDN 和扩散渲染器 RGB<->X 分别提升 +8.6 dB / +10.1 dB PSNR；在感知时间稳定性（temporal-LPIPS flicker）上提升 2.5-8 倍。质量接近路径追踪，但算力仅为后者一小部分；并非实时，以算力换取可控性与质量。

Conclusion: DAGS 通过解耦外观与几何条件、无注意力地注入冻结 DiT，实现了高保真、高忠实度且可独立控制的渲染，并以轻量时间组件支持流式渲染，在 PSNR 与时间稳定性上显著优于对比方法。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) generate high-fidelity images from text and image conditions, but their outputs carry large variance and their faithfulness to a desired target depends heavily on how the condition is supplied. We present DAGS, a lightweight, attention-free, disentangled appearance and geometry conditioning scheme that steers a frozen image DiT to produce high-fidelity, highly faithful, and independently controllable renders. Two small convolutional encoders compute conditioning features once per frame and inject them as a learned, per-layer, element-wise residual into the image tokens, avoiding the quadratic cost of stacking conditions through attention. Because control and temporal handling live outside the frozen backbone, we retain its vast pretrained prior and eliminate backbone-overfitting risk. We further add a small recurrent lighting stabilizer and a training-free temporal guidance term that, coupled with our conditioning, elevate a per-frame image model into a streaming renderer. DAGS produces controllable, high-quality renders at a fraction of the compute of path tracing; it is not real-time, trading compute for controllability and quality. On a matched 1-spp + G-buffer input, per-frame DAGS reconstructs +8.6 dB / +10.1 dB PSNR over the real-time denoiser Intel OIDN and the diffusion renderer RGB<->X while being 2.5-8x more temporally stable perceptually (temporal-LPIPS flicker).

</details>


### [17] [Physical AI Smart Spaces: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Multi-Camera 3D Perception in Smart Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02580)
*Yuxing Wang,Yizhou Wang,Anqi Li,Shuo Wang,Sameer Satish Pusegaonkar,Haoquan Liang,Jiajun Li,Shenxin Jiang,Jianhe Yuan,Shangru Li,Tongwei Dai,Zihao Chen,David C. Anastasiu,Sujit Biswas,Xunlei Wu,Zheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Physical AI Smart Spaces 是首个同时提供大规模、多类别、多相机 3D 感知数据的室内智能空间基准，涵盖合成生成、外观增强与真实世界 Sim2Real 评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以同时满足大规模、多类别与多相机 3D 感知需求，作者希望为室内智能空间（仓库、医院、零售等）提供统一的数据与评测标准。

Method: 构建包含 280+ 小时同步 1080p 视频、近 1800 台相机的数据集，提供多相机身份、2D/3D 边界框、相机标定与深度等自动标注；流程涵盖 Isaac Sim 合成生成、Cosmos Transfer 外观增强及真实仓库部署（VGGT 标定与 3D 标注界面）。

Result: 提出 3D 版 HOTA 指标，将 2D 框跟踪评测扩展到 3D 位置与 3D 框；并报告 AI City Challenge 排行榜上的经验基线，展示从单人 3D 定位跟踪到多类别 3D 框跟踪的演进。

Conclusion: 该基准统一了数据范围、标注与标定模式、生成流程、评测协议与官方评估系统（标准化提交格式与排行榜），为智能空间 3D 感知研究提供公开资源与评测平台。

Abstract: Physical AI Smart Spaces is, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark to simultaneously provide large-scale, multi-class, and multi-camera 3D perception data for indoor smart spaces. It contains over 280 hours of synchronized 1080p footage captured by nearly 1,800 cameras in warehouses, hospitals, retail venues, and similar settings, together with automatic annotations for multi-camera identities, 2D bounding boxes, 3D bounding boxes, camera calibration, and depth where available. The benchmark spans Isaac Sim synthetic generation, Cosmos Transfer appearance augmentation, and real-world Sim2Real evaluation. For the real-world target, we include two warehouse deployments with time-synchronized streams, automatic VGGT-based calibration, and a 3D labeling interface that projects world-frame 3D boxes into each view for cross-camera verification. We describe the dataset scope, annotation and calibration schema, generation workflow, benchmark protocols, and official evaluation system, which standardizes submission format, and leaderboard reporting. A central contribution is a 3D instantiation of Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA), extending the usual 2D box-based tracking evaluation to 3D locations and 3D boxes. We further report empirical baselines from the AI City Challenge leaderboards, showing how methods evolve from person-only 3D location tracking to multi-class 3D box tracking under realistic smart-space constraints. The release is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/nvidia/PhysicalAI-SmartSpaces.

</details>


### [18] [GRAFT: Growing Agglomerative Foundation Models via Continual Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02597)
*Zhenghao Zhao,Chi Zhang,Qingshuang Chen,Yelin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRAFT，一种持续式多教师知识蒸馏框架，使统一骨干网络能按顺序不断从新基础模型获取能力，无需对整个教师集合重复联合蒸馏，从而用单一骨干整合五个领域的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（如 DINOv2、SigLIP2、MASt3R）因预训练目标不同而具备互补能力，但知识分散在各自独立的模型中。多教师蒸馏可将这些能力整合进单一骨干，然而现有方法假定教师集合固定，一旦新增教师就需对整个教师集合重新联合蒸馏，代价高昂。

Method: GRAFT 将此前已蒸馏的模型作为教师以保留已学能力，当前学生同时向上一代模型与新到来的教师学习。为调和异构教师间不兼容的表示几何，引入“教师专用读出令牌”（Teacher Specific Readout Tokens），让每个教师对共享编码器进行独立读出；并提出“几何无关关系损失”（Geometry Agnostic Relational Loss），通过匹配图文相似度结构而非原始特征值来对齐视觉-语言教师。

Result: 在图像理解、2D 密集预测、3D 人体姿态估计、3D 视觉与视觉-语言五个领域上，GRAFT 均取得强性能，且每获取一项新能力仅需一次蒸馏，而非完整重新蒸馏。

Conclusion: GRAFT 提供了一个单一且可持续扩展的骨干网络，统一了五个领域的能力，实现了开放序列下的持续多教师蒸馏，显著降低了新增教师时的计算成本。

Abstract: Vision foundation models such as DINOv2, SigLIP2, and MASt3R develop complementary capabilities from different pretraining objectives, yet their knowledge remains distributed across separate, specialized models. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation offers a path toward consolidating these capabilities into a single agglomerative backbone, but existing approaches assume a fixed set of teachers, and incorporating a new teacher requires repeating expensive joint distillation over the entire teacher set. We introduce GRAFT, a continual multi-teacher distillation framework that enables a unified backbone to progressively acquire capabilities from an open-ended sequence of foundation models. When a new teacher arrives, GRAFT treats the previously distilled model as a teacher for preserving learned capabilities, while the current student jointly learns from both the previous model and the incoming teacher. Furthermore, to reconcile the incompatible representation geometries of heterogeneous teachers, we introduce Teacher Specific Readout Tokens, which grant each teacher an independent read-out of the shared encoder, together with Geometry Agnostic Relational Loss that aligns a vision-language teacher by matching image-text similarity structures rather than raw feature values. We provide GRAFT model, which is a single, continually extensible backbone that unifies five domains, including image understanding, 2D dense prediction, 3D human pose estimation, 3D vision, and vision-language, delivering strong performance across all of them while acquiring each new capability at the cost of a single distillation rather than a full re-distillation.

</details>


### [19] [Imagine the Future, Internalize the Gist: Efficient VLA Reasoning via Internalized Spatiotemporal Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02626)
*Shenglan Li,Zhendong Mi,Hengyi Zhu,Jingwu Luo,Chun Kit Chan,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang,Pu Zhao,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IG-VLA 提出一种视觉-语言-动作推理框架，让模型在视觉表征空间中进行潜在时空推理以“想象未来”，并通过场景要点记忆将其内化，从而在提升操作成功率的同时大幅降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽引入中间推理来改进机器人操作，但主要针对已观测状态进行推理，缺乏对未来场景演化的显式预判；而在每个推理步骤都做显式未来推演又会带来巨大计算开销。因此需要一种既能利用未来推理优势、又能保持推理高效的方案。

Method: 提出 IG-VLA 框架，包含两部分：(1) Latent Spatiotemporal Reasoning，直接在视觉表征空间学习想象与任务相关的未来场景演化，用于指导动作预测，避免昂贵的像素级视频生成；(2) Scene Gist Memory，将推理得到的场景-行为关联内化为紧凑的 Scene Gist Token，在推理阶段绕过显式未来想象，从而降低开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、VLABench 上验证有效性与效率。在 LIBERO-Plus Language 套件上，推理策略与要点策略均比最强基线成功率高出近 6%；要点策略相比基线最高实现 6.38 倍加速，单张 NVIDIA A6000 GPU 上每个动作块推理延迟从 1081ms 降至 169.5ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明未来时空推理可被有效内化，从而支持高效 VLA 部署，兼顾操作性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly incorporate intermediate reasoning to improve robotic manipulation, yet existing approaches primarily reason about observed states without explicitly anticipating future scene evolution. Extending such reasoning to explicit future rollouts at every inference step, however, introduces substantial computational overhead. We propose IG-VLA, a VLA reasoning framework that enables models to imagine the future and internalize the gist. Our Latent Spatiotemporal Reasoning learns to imagine task-relevant future scene evolution directly in visual representation space, guiding action prediction without costly pixel-level video generation. To further reduce inference overhead, we introduce Scene Gist Memory, which internalizes reasoning-derived scene-behavior associations into a compact Scene Gist Token, preserving the benefits of future reasoning while bypassing explicit future imagination at inference. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLABench demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of IG-VLA. On the LIBERO-Plus Language suite, both the reasoning and gist policies outperform the strongest baseline by nearly 6% in success rate. The gist policy also achieves up to 6.38x speedup over baselines, reducing inference latency from 1081ms to 169.5ms per action chunk on a single NVIDIA A6000 GPU. These results demonstrate that future spatiotemporal reasoning can be effectively internalized for efficient VLA deployment.

</details>


### [20] [Capturing Dynamics: The 4D Facial Expression Intensity Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02647)
*Zesheng Wang,Alexandre Bruckert,Pierre Lebreton,Patrick Le Callet,Yante Li,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4D面部表情强度数据集4DFEID，利用参数化人脸模型生成2,869个网格序列，并通过众包收集逾90,000条Likert量表主观强度评分，用于探究动态3D刺激下的表情强度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往表情强度研究多基于帧级2D表征，难以全面理解现实中本质为3D且时间连续的面部表情，缺乏动态3D刺激下的强度感知资源。

Method: 采用参数化人脸模型构建2,869个受控几何变化的网格序列，生成具有不同峰值强度和身份属性的4D数据实例；通过众包平台以Likert量表收集超过90,000条主观强度评分；并探索多种架构与聚合方法建立情节强度估计基线。

Result: 建立了新数据集上的情节强度估计基线，发现时空图模型持续优于传统的帧聚合方法。

Conclusion: 4DFEID提供高保真、时空连贯的4D面部数据，为动态3D刺激下的表情强度感知研究提供独特资源，弥补了情感计算与人机交互领域的研究空白。

Abstract: The estimation and analysis of facial expression intensity play a crucial role in affective communication and human-computer interaction. Previous research has primarily focused on detecting and estimating facial expression intensity from frame-level 2D representations. However, this limitation restricts a comprehensive understanding of real-world facial expressions, as they are inherently 3D and temporally continuous. This paper investigates the perception of facial expression intensity by introducing the 4D Facial Expression Intensity Dataset (4DFEID). We employ a parametric face model and compile a total of 2,869 mesh sequences with controlled geometric variations, generating 4D data instances with diverse peak intensities and identity attributes. Using a Likert scale, we collect more than 90,000 subjective intensity perception ratings via a crowdsourcing platform. We explore various architectures and aggregation methods to establish baselines for episode intensity estimation on the new dataset, revealing that spatial-temporal graph models consistently outperform traditional frame-aggregation methods. In contrast to existing datasets that rely on 2D static imagery, the proposed 4D-FEID dataset provides the community with a unique and vital resource for investigating the perception of facial expression intensity through the use of dynamic 3D stimuli. By offering high-fidelity, spatio-temporally coherent facial data, 4D-FEID establishes a new foundation for research into more nuanced and naturalistic expression analysis, thereby addressing a gap in the current landscape of affective computing and human-computer interaction studies. The dataset is available at link.

</details>


### [21] [SpectralCache: Accelerating Diffusion-Based World Models via Spectral Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02660)
*Zhendong Mi,Pu Zhao,Ziyu Hu,Xiaodong Yu,Yanzhi Wang,Grace Li Zhang,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpectralCache，一种免训练的谱域缓存框架，通过复用稳定的奇异子空间并线性外推奇异值，加速扩散世界模型推理，在 HunyuanWorld-Voyager-13B 上实现 5.22 倍加速且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型虽能生成高质量交互式环境，但因去噪过程中反复进行 Transformer 评估而推理开销巨大。现有缓存方法仅在特征或 token 层面利用时间冗余，未挖掘扩散特征背后的数学结构。

Method: 研究发现相邻去噪步的世界模型特征具有高度稳定的奇异子空间，且奇异值呈可预测的演化规律。基于此提出 SpectralCache：免训练地复用稳定奇异子空间，仅对低维奇异值进行线性外推估计；并利用相邻完整计算特征间的谱一致性，通过奇异值缩放跳过部分昂贵的骨干网络评估。

Result: 在代表性世界模型上，SpectralCache 在保持生成质量的同时持续提升推理效率。在 HunyuanWorld-Voyager-13B 上实现 5.22 倍加速，静态场景 WorldScore 达 65.90，推理效率显著优于现有免训练缓存方法。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘扩散特征的谱结构（稳定奇异子空间与可预测奇异值演化），SpectralCache 为世界模型提供了一种高效的免训练加速方案，兼顾推理速度与生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion-based world models enable high-quality interactive environment generation but suffer from substantial inference overhead due to repeated Transformer evaluations during denoising. Existing caching methods mainly exploit temporal redundancy at the feature or token level, leaving the underlying mathematical structure of diffusion features largely unexplored. In this work, we reveal that world-model features exhibit highly stable singular subspaces across nearby denoising steps, while their singular values follow predictable evolution patterns. Building on this observation, we propose SpectralCache, a training-free spectral caching framework that reuses stable singular subspaces and estimates only low-dimensional singular values through linear extrapolation. We further exploit the spectral consistency between neighboring full-computation features to skip selected expensive backbone evaluations via singular value scaling. Extensive experiments on representative world models demonstrate that SpectralCache consistently improves inference efficiency while preserving generation quality. On HunyuanWorld-Voyager-13B, SpectralCache achieves 5.22x acceleration while maintaining a WorldScore of 65.90 for static scenes, substantially outperforming existing training-free caching methods in inference efficiency.

</details>


### [22] [CHASE-VLA: Post-Training Quantization Framework for Vision-Language-Action Models with Chunk-Aware Scale Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02666)
*Jin Hyun,Jung Gyu Min,Gyuhyun Jung,Youngjoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASE-VLA，一种面向扩散式动作专家（AE）的块感知训练后量化方法，利用生成的动作块作为因果上下文自适应调整激活尺度，实现W4A4量化并恢复FP16性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉观测与语言指令映射为连续机器人动作，但其中基于扩散的动作专家在低比特训练后量化（PTQ）上面临挑战：AE在去噪步骤和策略查询中被反复调用，固定的校准尺度难以匹配随去噪进度和目标运动而变化的激活动态范围。

Method: CHASE-VLA利用策略中现成的VLA特有信号——生成的动作块（包括未执行的未来后缀）。它不局限于AE层的静态尺度匹配，而是将先前生成的动作块作为因果动作上下文，结合去噪步骤分组信息来自适应调整AE激活尺度，从而在不修改预训练策略的前提下对AE中的MLP和注意力投影实现W4A4量化。

Result: 在LIBERO上，对π0.5的AE中MLP与注意力投影均量化为W4A4时，CHASE-VLA取得97.3%的平均成功率，恢复FP16水平性能；量化后AE线性层权重存储减少73.4%，单块内存访问量在π0.5与GR00T N1.6上分别减少70.9%和71.2%，而预测器开销最多仅为节省存储的1.26%。

Conclusion: CHASE-VLA通过块感知的自适应尺度策略，有效解决了扩散式动作专家低比特量化的激活失配问题，在几乎不损失精度的同时显著降低存储与内存访问开销，为VLA模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions to continuous robot actions, but a diffusion-based action expert (AE) poses a key challenge for low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ). The AE is repeatedly invoked across denoising steps and policy queries, where fixed calibration scales can be mismatched with activation ranges that vary with denoising progress and intended motion. We propose CHASE-VLA, a chunk-aware PTQ method that exploits a VLA-specific signal readily available from the policy: the generated action chunk, including its unexecuted future suffix. Rather than relying only on static scale matching for AE layers, CHASE-VLA combines the previously generated chunk as causal action context with denoising step group information to adapt AE activation scales. This enables W4A4 quantization of both MLP and attention projections in the repeated AE without modifying the pretrained policy. On LIBERO, CHASE-VLA achieves 97.3% average success rate on $π_{0.5}$ when both MLP and attention projections in the AE are quantized to W4A4, restoring FP16-level performance. CHASE-VLA also reduces the weight storage of the quantized AE linear layers by 73.4% and their single-chunk memory traffic by 70.9% and 71.2% on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.6, respectively, with a predictor overhead of at most 1.26% of the saved storage.

</details>


### [23] [Revisiting Visual Representation Enhancement of VLMs via Kernel Canonical Correlation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02718)
*Peilin Yang,Xiaoyu Liu,Jian Sun,Qinghua Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于KCCA的特征子空间对齐方法3vKCCA，通过KKT条件实现端到端优化，避免特征值问题，并扩展为三视图联合对齐，在CLIP ViT-L/14上显著提升细粒度视觉感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型虽具强语义泛化能力，但细粒度视觉感知不足。已有工作KUEA通过DINOv2监督逐元素对齐图像编码器核矩阵来改善，但作者发现降低该对齐损失权重并不必然损害细粒度性能，说明核矩阵差异可能不足以充分提升视觉表征，因此需要重新审视对齐公式。

Method: 从特征子空间角度重新刻画表征对齐，采用核典型相关分析（KCCA）最大化投影相关性；在优化上基于KKT条件推导高效端到端训练方案，避免求解KCCA的特征值问题；进一步扩展为三视图形式3vKCCA，将预训练文本编码器的投影也纳入统一优化框架进行联合对齐。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上使用CLIP ViT-L/14时，3vKCCA将MMVP-VLM准确率从17.8提升至25.9，大幅优于现有方法，同时保持零样本图像-文本检索性能。

Conclusion: 以KCCA进行特征子空间对齐并扩展为三视图联合优化，能有效增强CLIP的细粒度视觉感知，同时不牺牲其原有的图像-文本语义对齐与检索能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP exhibit strong semantic generalization, but remain limited in fine-grained visual perception. A recent work named KUEA presents a natural remedy by finetuning the image encoder under the supervision of the vision-centric DINOv2 to align their kernel matrices element-wisely, while regularizing the embeddings to remain close to the pretrained visual encoder for preserving image-text semantics in CLIP. However, we show that diminishing the role of the alignment loss to DINOv2 does not necessarily degrade its fine-grained visual performance, suggesting that the kernel-matrix discrepancy may be insufficient for further visual representation enhancement, motivating us to revisit the alignment formulation. In this work, we present a novel perspective to characterize representation alignment on feature subspaces through Kernel Canonical Correlation Analysis (KCCA), which maximizes the projection correlations. In optimization, we derive an efficient end-to-end training scheme upon KKT conditions, avoiding the eigenvalue problem in KCCA. Further, we extend our method into a 3-view formulation, i.e., 3vKCCA, in which the projections from the pretrained text encoder are also incorporated under a unified optimization framework for joint alignment. With CLIP ViT-L/14 on ImageNet-1K, our 3vKCCA improves the MMVP-VLM accuracy from 17.8 to 25.9, substantially outperforming the existing methods, and meanwhile maintains zero-shot image--text retrieval performance.

</details>


### [24] [SymRegFlow: Symmetry-Regularized Flow Matching for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02726)
*Xi Ye,Yuzhu Wang,Xiaoyang Liu,Jiayi Wang,Yangyang Xu,Ruyu Wang,Wenlin Chen,Duo Su,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SymRegFlow，一种对称正则化流匹配框架，在无真值新视角 RGB 监督下实现连续视点、多视角一致的视频生成，并在 nuScenes 上取得最低 FVD/FVMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流匹配的多视角世界模型虽能生成逼真视频，但通常局限于固定相机装置；扩展到连续变化相机位姿需要昂贵且难获取的稠密位姿覆盖配对位姿-视频数据。

Method: 对每个目标位姿，将源视角几何变换为带噪锚点，结合掩码双锚点监督与跨锚点去噪输出一致性，以缓解锚点特有误差；在仿射高斯替代模型下证明合适的一致性正则可在固定噪声水平下恢复干净参考最优解。

Result: 在 Cosmos-Drive-Dreams 和 nuScenes 上实现高质量、多视角一致的自动驾驶视频生成；nuScenes 上 FVD 和 FVMD 最低，FVD 较最佳基线降低超 31%，源条件推理的 FID 与实例保持也最优。

Conclusion: SymRegFlow 通过对称正则化与双锚点一致性，在无需新视角 RGB 真值监督下实现连续视点一致的多视角视频生成，理论上优于单锚点与合并锚点基线，实验上刷新多项指标，为可扩展的多视角世界模型提供新思路。

Abstract: Flow-matching-based multi-view world models generate realistic videos, but are commonly restricted to fixed camera rigs. Extending them to continuously varying camera poses requires paired pose--video observations with dense pose coverage, which are costly to acquire. We introduce \emph{SymRegFlow}, a symmetry-regularized flow-matching framework for multi-view-consistent video generation across continuous viewpoints without ground-truth novel-view RGB supervision. For each target pose, SymRegFlow geometrically warps source views into noisy anchors and combines masked dual-anchor supervision with cross-anchor denoising-output consistency to mitigate anchor-specific errors. Under an affine Gaussian surrogate, we prove that suitable consistency regularization recovers the clean-reference optimum at fixed noise levels, strictly outperforming single- and merged-anchor baselines. Experiments on Cosmos-Drive-Dreams and nuScenes demonstrate high-quality, multi-view-consistent autonomous-driving video generation: on nuScenes, SymRegFlow achieves the lowest FVD and FVMD among the evaluated baselines, reducing FVD by over 31\% relative to the best baseline, and source-conditioned inference also attains the best FID and instance preservation.

</details>


### [25] [Correcting Guided Diffusion Trajectories with Spectral Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02753)
*Gihoon Kim,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱校正引导（Spectral Correction Guidance），通过频谱对齐准则理解并改进无分类器引导（CFG）的采样行为，无需训练即可提升条件图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件图像生成的成功依赖条件对齐与视觉保真度的细粒度差异，而核心的无分类器引导（CFG）缺乏显式判据，难以评估引导轨迹是否符合预期。

Method: 分析发现中间状态的频谱可作为与正向过程预期频谱演化一致性的指标；据此提出频谱校正引导，在采样过程中对偏离解析参考频谱的偏差进行校正。该方法免训练，适用于多种扩散骨干网络和条件生成任务，无需修改底层模型。

Result: 在文本到图像生成中，基于偏好的指标相较基线引导方法取得一致提升；在 ImageNet 上相较 CFG 生成质量更优。这些改进在多种引导尺度和更少去噪步数下依然保持。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了引导行为的机制，并说明所提方法如何影响生成质量，为改进引导扩散采样提供了有原则的频谱视角。

Abstract: The practical success of conditional image generation hinges on fine-grained differences in condition alignment and visual fidelity. Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is central to this success, but its lack of an explicit criterion makes it difficult to assess whether the guided trajectory is progressing as intended. To address this gap, we show that spectral alignment provides a principled criterion for understanding guidance behavior and improving guided diffusion sampling through adaptive correction. Our analysis identifies the spectra of intermediate states as an indicator of consistency with the expected spectral evolution of the forward process. Based on this observation, we introduce Spectral Correction Guidance, a method that corrects deviations from an analytic reference spectrum during sampling. The proposed method is training-free and applicable across diffusion backbones and conditional generation tasks without modifying the underlying model. Experiments demonstrate consistent gains in preference-based metrics over baseline guidance methods in text-to-image generation and improved generation quality over CFG on ImageNet. These improvements persist across a range of guidance scales and with fewer denoising steps. Our analyses and ablations provide insight into guidance behavior and how the proposed method affects generation quality.

</details>


### [26] [FiberGeoText: A Vision-Language Model for Population- Level Organization of Superficial White Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02755)
*Yuqian Chen,R. Jarrett Rushmore,Guikun Chen,Fan Zhang,Edward Yeterian,Nikos Makris,Yogesh Rathi,Lauren J. O'Donnell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FiberGeoText (FGT) 视觉-语言模型，将超高清扩散MRI重建的短程浅表白质( SWM )纤维流线组织为群体水平聚类，融合轨迹、皮层解剖上下文与形状三方面信息，性能显著优于SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅表白质含大量短程联络纤维，对认知与脑疾病至关重要，但其组织方式尚未充分刻画。困难在于纤维轨迹短、皮层折叠高度可变，导致个体间对应关系难以建立；解剖学上对应的连接在空间位置上可能因人而异，仅靠几何邻近性无法充分定义。

Method: 提出FGT视觉-语言模型，联合表征每条流线的三维轨迹、皮层解剖上下文与形状。将多套皮层分区方案得到的皮层端点信息转化为文本，用预训练大语言模型编码，使异构解剖描述汇聚到统一连续表征中。在0.76 mm亚毫米扩散MRI数据上评估。

Result: 与SOTA方法相比，FGT在皮层分区一致性、簇内形状一致性、簇大小一致性及跨被试对应性上均有显著提升；模型对未见被试泛化良好，平均恢复5,000个已学簇中的96.7%，训练与测试数据的簇结构高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过VLM学习多模态深度嵌入，整合几何、解剖与形状信息，可在存在个体间解剖变异的情况下稳健学习群体一致的SWM组织。

Abstract: The superficial white matter (SWM), a critical brain region for cognition across the lifespan and brain disease, contains abundant short-range association fibers whose organization remains incompletely characterized, in part because the short trajectories and highly variable cortical folding make correspondence across individuals challenging. Anatomically corresponding connections may vary in spatial location across individuals and therefore may not be adequately defined by geometric proximity alone. We introduce FiberGeoText (FGT), a vision-language model (VLM) for organizing short-range superficial white matter (SWM) streamlines reconstructed from ultra-high-resolution diffusion MRI into population-level clusters. FGT jointly represents three complementary properties of each streamline: its three-dimensional trajectory, its cortical anatomical context, and its shape. Cortical endpoint information from multiple parcellation schemes is expressed as text and encoded using a pretrained large language model (LLM), enabling heterogeneous anatomical descriptions to contribute to a common continuous representation. We evaluated FGT on acquired submillimeter 0.76 mm diffusion MRI data. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, FGT produced substantially greater cortical parcel coherence, within-cluster shape consistency, cluster-size consistency, and cross-subject correspondence. The trained model also generalizes well to unseen subjects with an average of 96.7% of the 5,000 learned clusters recovered, and high consistency of cluster structure between training and testing data. Together, these findings demonstrate that integrating geometric, anatomical, and shape information by learning multimodal deep embeddings with a VLM model enables robust learning of population-consistent SWM organization despite interindividual anatomical variability.

</details>


### [27] [TRAC: Trajectory-aware Reuse and Adaptive Correction for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02779)
*Jiaxing Song,Weiqi Yan,You Huang,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRAC：一种免训练的自回归视频生成加速框架，通过缓存复用调度与自适应校正，在SkyReels-V2和FramePack-F1上实现最高推理效率与最佳生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速方法主要针对采用双向注意力的单轨迹生成，而自回归（AR）视频生成按顺序耦合块级去噪轨迹，导致近似误差在生成过程中累积和传播，因此需要专门针对AR特性的加速与校正方案。

Method: TRAC包含三个组件：稳健累积调度（RCS），依据累积展开误差与跨块/提示变化来选择缓存复用调度；自回归轨迹感知引导调度（ATGS），沿全局AR轨迹协调CFG刷新；频谱结构校正（SSC），恢复首个块的低频结构以校正长期结构损失。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在SkyReels-V2和FramePack-F1上的实验表明，与现有方法相比，TRAC在自回归视频生成中同时取得最高的推理效率和最佳的生成质量。

Conclusion: TRAC通过轨迹感知的缓存复用与自适应校正，有效缓解AR视频生成中的误差累积问题，是一种高效的免训练加速框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we present trajectory-aware reuse and adaptive correction (TRAC), a training-free framework for efficient autoregressive (AR) video generation. Existing acceleration methods mainly target single-trajectory generation with bidirectional attention. AR video generation, by contrast, sequentially couples chunk-level denoising trajectories. Consequently, approximation errors accumulate and propagate through the generation process. TRAC addresses this challenge with three components, including robust cumulative scheduling (RCS), autoregressive trajectory-aware guidance scheduling (ATGS), and spectral structure correction (SSC). RCS selects cache reuse schedules by cumulative rollout error and cross-chunk/prompt variation. ATGS coordinates CFG refreshes along the global AR trajectory. SSC restores low-frequency structure of the first chunk to correct long-term structural loss. Experiments on SkyReels-V2 and FramePack-F1 show that, compared with existing methods, TRAC achieves both the highest inference efficiency and the best generation quality for AR video generation.

</details>


### [28] [FUSEye: Training-Light Fisheye Detection with Overlapping Views and Zero-Initialized Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02799)
*Wenya Su,Kai Luo,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUSEye是一个轻量训练框架，将冻结骨干的COCO预训练YOLO26-x检测器适配到鱼眼图像，通过GridViews、Z-Adapters和AgreeFusion三个层次解决畸变与边界压缩问题，仅新增约227k参数，在WoodScape基准上将mAP50从0.148提升到0.266。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机为移动机器人提供低成本单传感器环视能力，但常用的COCO预训练检测器在其上表现很差：强径向畸变扭曲局部图像结构，边界压缩使物体缩小到近乎不可见。全面微调虽能缩小差距，但需要大量鱼眼标注和计算资源，因此需要一种训练轻量化的迁移方案。

Method: FUSEye冻结COCO预训练的YOLO26-x骨干，仅新增约227k参数，更新插入模块和预训练检测头，从三个因果关联层次解决迁移差距：(1) 输入层——重叠网格视图生成与框重映射（GridViews），放大被压缩的边界区域；(2) 特征层——零初始化残差适配器（Z-Adapters），校正畸变引起的特征错位；(3) 决策层——学习式跨投影一致性融合（AgreeFusion），仅在多视图证据一致时提升低置信度检测。

Result: 在WoodScape环视鱼眼基准上，FUSEye将YOLO26-x的mAP50从0.148提升至0.266，保留全面微调精度的84.3%。仅随机使用25%标注训练图像时，达到0.2597 mAP50，保留全标注性能的97.6%。此外，FUSEye在YOLOv8-11系列检测器上均稳定提升，表明该方案与架构无关。

Conclusion: FUSEye是一种训练轻量、架构无关的鱼眼检测适配框架，通过输入、特征、决策三级协同设计，在极少参数和少量标注条件下显著缩小了COCO预训练检测器与鱼眼域之间的迁移差距，具有良好的实用性和可推广性。

Abstract: Fisheye cameras give mobile robots a single-sensor, low-cost view of their surroundings, yet the COCO-pretrained detectors that practitioners routinely reuse fail on them: strong radial distortion warps local image structure, while boundary compression shrinks objects to near-invisible sizes. Full fine-tuning closes much of the gap but requires abundant fisheye labels and compute. We present FUSEye, a training-light framework that turns a frozen-backbone COCO-pretrained extra-large YOLO26 detector (YOLO26-x) into a fisheye detector. FUSEye adds roughly 227k new parameters while updating the inserted modules and the pretrained detection head. It addresses the transfer gap at three causally linked levels. At the input level, overlapping grid view generation and box remapping (GridViews) enlarge compressed boundary regions. At the feature level, zero-initialized residual adapters (Z-Adapters) correct distortion-induced feature misalignment. At the decision level, learned cross-projection agreement fusion (AgreeFusion) promotes low-confidence detections only when they are supported by consistent evidence across multiple views. On the WoodScape surround-view fisheye benchmark, FUSEye raises YOLO26-x from 0.148 to 0.266 mAP50 and retains 84.3% fully fine-tuned accuracy. Moreover, randomly using only 25% of the labeled training images, FUSEye achieves 0.2597 mAP50, retaining 97.6% of its full-label performance. FUSEye also consistently improves YOLOv8-11 detectors, showing that the recipe is architecture-agnostic. Source code will be available at https://github.com/Su-wenya/FUSEye.

</details>


### [29] [TerrainForge: Physics-Grounded road geometry Editing for Counterfactual Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02825)
*Yang Chen,Yicheng Zhu,zhenning Li,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 TerrainForge 框架，可从重建的多车驾驶片段生成以道路几何为核心的反事实场景，并训练用于快速筛选道路编辑候选的代理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路几何（坡顶、坡谷、减速带等）和路面条件（湿滑、结冰等）会同时影响车辆运动、驾驶员与车载相机视野以及车辆间距。若在驾驶场景中编辑这些属性，必须同步改变车辆运动；但驾驶视频中要捕捉这些差异，需要道路编辑能传播到车辆运动、相机视角和车辆间距。因此需要一种能统一建模道路与多车动态、并评估安全后果的方法。

Method: TerrainForge 从重建的多车驾驶片段出发生成道路几何反事实。它使用统一道路模型连接场景变形与四轮车辆动力学，使坡顶、坡谷、减速带和摩擦变化能够传播到车辆运动、相机视角和车间距。车辆动力学与 CarSim 对照验证，规定的道路几何在重建 Waymo 场景中验证。在 983 个制动片段上模拟 ego 对 15,758 次道路编辑的响应，并与未编辑回放的安全结果配对，训练第一阶段筛选代理模型：输入原始驾驶上下文和候选道路编辑参数，预测 ego 终端间距的变化。

Result: 在 18 个片段中，若让周围车辆保持记录轨迹而不重新计算其响应，预测的 ego-前车间距中位峰值差异在坡顶为 1.52 m、坡谷为 1.41 m。代理模型在留出场景上的预测平均绝对误差比“预测无变化”低 22-40%。

Conclusion: TerrainForge 能将道路几何编辑一致地传播到多车运动、相机视角与车间距，并支持低成本筛选道路编辑候选，在完整多车 rollout 前快速评估安全影响。

Abstract: Road geometry (e.g., crests, sags, and speed humps) and surface conditions (e.g., wet or icy pavement) affect how vehicles move, what drivers and onboard cameras observe, and how much clearance remains between vehicles. Editing these properties in a driving scene therefore requires corresponding changes in vehicle motion. Capturing these differences in a driving video requires a road edit to propagate to vehicle motion, camera viewpoint, and the clearance between vehicles. We present TerrainForge, a framework for generating road geometry-focused counterfactuals from reconstructed multi-vehicle driving episodes. A unified road model connects scene deformation with four-wheel vehicle dynamics, allowing crests, sags, speed humps, and friction changes to propagate through vehicle motion, camera viewpoint, and inter-vehicle clearance. Vehicle dynamics are evaluated against CarSim, and prescribed road geometry is verified in reconstructed Waymo scenes. Across 18 episodes, leaving surrounding vehicles on their recorded trajectories instead of recomputing their responses produces median peak differences in predicted ego-lead distance of 1.52 m for crests and 1.41 m for sags. We further simulate the ego response to 15,758 road edits across 983 braking episodes, pairing each edit with its safety outcomes relative to an unedited replay. These pairs train a first-stage screening surrogate that takes the original driving context and candidate road-edit parameters as input and predicts the resulting change in the ego's terminal gap. On held-out scenes, this prediction achieves 22-40% lower mean absolute error than predicting no change, so candidates can be screened cheaply before the full multi-vehicle rollout.

</details>


### [30] [Seeing, Saying, but Not Using: From Reportable Spatial Facts to Usable States in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02876)
*Jinchang Zhang,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SpaceConflict 基准（23,196 条输入），揭示多模态大模型存在"可用性—利用性缺口"：模型能从视觉证据恢复初始空间状态，却无法在后续变换推理中使用该状态；并提出 Operational State Supervision (OSS) 训练方法加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型能正确陈述某个空间事实，并不意味着它会在后续推理中真正使用这一事实。论文旨在区分"能否看到并陈述空间事实"与"该事实能否成为后续计算中可用的状态"这两件事，并系统研究这一差异。

Method: 1) 构建 SpaceConflict 基准，包含 23,196 条输入，采用统一的 Supported/Contradictory/Unknown 判定接口，覆盖四个层级：L1 局部事实绑定、L2 关系组合、L3 跨观察一致性、L4 变换下的状态判定。2) 对同一世界分别提出直接状态查询、完整变换查询和显式初始状态查询，以暴露可用性—利用性缺口。3) 提出 Operational State Supervision (OSS)，监督任务相关空间状态及其变换轨迹，并对齐跨上下文中的共享事实。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-9B 上，100 条正确恢复初始状态的序列中有 50 条在完整变换任务上失败；将状态显式提供后，这 50 条全部被修复。该缺口随模型规模增大而缩小，但并未完全消失。OSS 在匹配判定上的配对准确率提升，在依赖组织与使用状态的 L3、L4 层级上提升最为明显。

Conclusion: 评估多模态空间推理时，不应只问模型能否看到并陈述某个空间事实，还要问该事实能否在后续计算中成为可用的状态。OSS 表明通过监督状态与变换轨迹可有效缩小可用性—利用性缺口。

Abstract: A multimodal large language model that correctly reports a spatial fact does not necessarily use that fact in subsequent reasoning. To study this distinction, we introduce \textsc{SpaceConflict}, a benchmark of 23{,}196 inputs for the construction and use of spatial state. Under a unified Supported/Contradictory/Unknown judgment interface, it covers local fact binding (L1), relational composition (L2), cross-observation consistency (L3), and state judgment under transformation (L4). Posing a direct-state query, a full-transformation query, and an explicit-initial-state query on the same world reveals an availability--utilization gap: models recover the initial state from visual evidence yet fail when that state must drive a transformation. For Qwen3.5-9B, 50 of 100 sequences with a correctly recovered initial state fail the full transformation, and supplying the state explicitly repairs all 50; the gap narrows with scale but does not close. We therefore propose Operational State Supervision (OSS), which supervises task-relevant spatial states and their transformation trajectories and aligns shared facts across contexts. OSS improves paired accuracy on matched judgments most on L3 and L4, the levels that depend on organizing and using state. Evaluating multimodal spatial reasoning thus requires asking not only whether a model can see and state a spatial fact, but whether that fact becomes a usable state in subsequent computation.

</details>


### [31] [Found but Not Read: When Extracted Text Closes the Retrieval-Reading Gap in Document Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02880)
*Qingtao Xia,Siyao Cheng,Jiahua Bao,Jiaxing Du,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示检索增强文档问答中存在"检索-阅读鸿沟"：证据页被检索到，但VLM无法在页内定位并使用该证据；加入OCR文本可将严格准确率提升13-16个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强文档问答假设"只要找到正确页面，VLM就能读懂"，但作者发现该假设常常失效，形成"证据已找到却未被使用"的检索-阅读鸿沟，因此需要一套配对协议来隔离并量化这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出配对协议，对比两种输入：仅检索到的页面图像 vs. 同一图像加上其提取文本；并构建带可追溯证据的FoveDoc-Bench基准，在六个VLM（三个家族）上做对照实验，同时用裁剪、边界框、图表证据、检索退化、无关文本等设置探测边界。

Result: 检索几乎能找到所有证据页，但加入CPU-OCR文本使严格准确率提升13-16个百分点，精确文本层约翻倍；增益在六个VLM上普遍存在，同家族内随规模缩小但不消失。证据裁剪可恢复大部分文本增益，页面上的边界框则无效；提取文本对文本类证据有效，对图表图像中性或有害；其优势随检索质量下降而缩小，同形式的无关文本无显著作用。

Conclusion: VLM能读却找不到页内证据，提取文本的作用是放大有效检索，而非替代失效的检索。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented document question answering assumes that once the right page is found, a vision-language model (VLM) can read it. We show that this assumption often fails, leaving a retrieval-reading gap: evidence found but not used. A paired protocol isolates this gap by comparing answers from the retrieved page images alone with answers from the same images plus their extracted text. On FoveDoc-Bench, our benchmark with traceable evidence, retrieval finds nearly every evidence page, yet adding CPU-OCR text raises strict accuracy by 13 to 16 points. An exact text layer roughly doubles the gain, which appears across six VLMs from three families and, within one family, narrows with scale without closing. The reader can read this evidence but cannot find it: crops of it recover most of the text gain, boxes around it on the page none. The same protocol identifies two boundaries. Extracted text helps on textual evidence but is neutral or harmful on charts and figures. Its advantage shrinks as retrieval degrades, and unrelated text of the same form adds nothing detectable. Extracted text is an amplifier of retrieval that works, not a substitute for retrieval that does not. Our code is available at https://github.com/atoz03/fovedoc-sup.

</details>


### [32] [Revealing Epistemic Uncertainty in MLLMs via Causal-Invariant Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02887)
*Haoyang Luo,Linwei Tao,Jie Gui,Xinghao Chen,Chang Xu,Jianyuan Guo,Minjing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态大模型（MLLMs）幻觉问题，提出因果不变掩码（CIM）框架与语义散度指标进行不确定性量化（UQ），并引入快速几何代理指标EED，实现SOTA性能与近50%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs存在幻觉，部署时亟需不确定性量化（UQ）以保障可靠性。但现有方法难以检测由表层关联引起的不确定性，尤其当查询相关信号较弱时。作者认为根源在于这些方法偏向由数据歧义带来的偶然不确定性（aleatoric），而忽视了源于模型局限性的认知不确定性（epistemic）。

Method: 提出因果不变掩码（CIM），通过度量原始预测与在“因果聚焦视图”条件下的预测之间的语义偏移来分解不确定性类型；基于该框架提出核心UQ指标“语义散度”（Semantic Divergence）；并进一步提出快速几何代理指标“期望嵌入漂移”（EED），在超球嵌入空间中直接估计语义偏移以加速UQ。

Result: 理论上证明了语义散度收敛于模型对非因果相关性敏感度的方差，说明其能捕捉MLLM的局限性；实验表明该方法在多个基准上取得SOTA性能，且EED在性能相当的情况下加速近50%。

Conclusion: CIM框架与语义散度指标能更全面地量化MLLM不确定性，侧重捕捉认知不确定性；EED作为高效代理可在保证性能的同时显著加速不确定性量化。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) suffer from hallucinations, creating a critical need for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) to ensure reliable deployment. However, existing approaches struggle to detect uncertainty caused by superficial associations, especially when the query-relevant signal is weak. We mainly attribute this issue to their bias toward aleatoric uncertainty arising from data ambiguity, overlooking epistemic uncertainty stemming from model limitations. To further decompose uncertainty types for a comprehensive UQ, we propose Causal-Invariant Masking (CIM), which measures the semantic shift between the original predictions and those conditioned on a causally-focused view. Based on this framework, we introduce Semantic Divergence as our core metric for UQ and provide theoretical evidence that it converges to the variance of model's sensitivity to non-causal correlations, establishing its ability to capture MLLM's limitation. To accelerate UQ in MLLMs, we further propose Expected Embedding Drift (EED), a fast geometric proxy metric that estimates semantic shift directly within the hyperspherical embedding space. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on various benchmarks, while the proposed EED accelerates by nearly 50% with comparable performance.

</details>


### [33] [ViTok: Improving Dense Semantics in AM-RADIO-Style Multi-Teacher Distillation with PHI-S and Masked Image Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02903)
*Hailun Xu,Kanchan Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文总结了一套多教师蒸馏配方，将SigLIP2（全局语义）与DINOv3-L（密集特征）蒸馏到AM-RADIO式学生模型中，通过一系列改进使ImageNet-1K kNN略超教师，并用PHI-S恢复ADE20K密集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是解决多教师蒸馏中"配方无法同时优化所有目标"的经验性问题：提升ImageNet-1K kNN精度的改动可能反而损害ADE20K分割性能，因此需要一套能显式管理全局识别与密集语义之间权衡的蒸馏方案。

Method: 采用AM-RADIO式学生，从SigLIP2与DINOv3-L双教师蒸馏。具体改进包括：为CLS与patch token拆分adaptor heads、非对称cosine/MSE损失、从DINOv3-L检查点初始化、教师重新加权、掩码图像建模（MIM）以及PHI-S特征平衡。

Result: 模型达到83.2 patch kNN与85.2 CLS kNN，在ImageNet-1K kNN分类上略超DINOv3-L教师；PHI-S将ADE20K的mIoU/mAcc从46.5/58.1提升至48.5/61.0，在该密集基准上匹配教师。负面结果：从ImageNet-1K扩展到ImageNet22K并不稳定增益，朴素加入SAM3或HOG等额外教师会引入干扰。

Conclusion: 论文不主张给出最终配方，而是将项目现状提炼为紧凑的经验性叙述与具体教训，供后续迭代参考，强调全局识别与密集语义的权衡需要显式管理与特征平衡。

Abstract: We study how to consolidate the current VITOK progress into a single multi-teacher distillation recipe that jointly preserves global recognition and dense semantics. Our starting point is an AM-RADIO-style student distilled from SigLIP2 and DINOv3-L, where SigLIP2 supplies strong global semantics and DINOv3-L supplies stronger dense features. The central empirical issue is that the same recipe does not optimize all objectives equally well: changes that improve ImageNet-1K kNN accuracy can still degrade ADE20K segmentation. We summarize a progression of modifications that make this trade-off more explicit and more manageable: split adaptor heads for CLS and patch tokens, asymmetric cosine/MSE losses, initialization from a DINOv3-L checkpoint, teacher reweighting, masked image modeling (MIM), and PHI-S feature balancing. The resulting model reaches 83.2 patch kNN and 85.2 CLS kNN, slightly surpassing the DINOv3-L teacher on ImageNet-1K kNN classification, while PHI-S restores ADE20K performance from 46.5/58.1 to 48.5/61.0 mIoU/mAcc, matching the teacher on this dense benchmark. We also summarize negative results: scaling distillation from ImageNet-1K to ImageNet22K does not consistently help, and naively adding extra teachers such as SAM3 or HOG features introduces interference. Rather than claiming a final recipe, this paper distills the current project state into a compact empirical story and a concrete set of lessons for future iterations.

</details>


### [34] [Custom Forcing: Training-Free Subject Customization for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02914)
*Yunseung Ok,Hyunsoo Kim,Minseo Kim,Suhyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Custom Forcing，一种无需训练的自回归视频定制方法，通过将参考锚帧存入冻结模型的持久 KV 缓存，并结合漂移自适应值放大（DVA）与锚对比引导（ACG），实现长视频中特定主体的稳定生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型能实时生成长视频，但只能根据文本生成泛化主体，无法从用户图像生成特定主体。现有定制方法要么需昂贵的逐主体优化，要么使用双向注意力的预训练条件网络，均不适配因果流式生成。

Method: 提出 Custom Forcing：无需训练，将基于参考的锚帧存入冻结自回归视频模型的持久 KV 缓存。针对固定锚帧的两个问题（身份漂移、文本提示偏向泛化主体），分别引入漂移自适应值放大（DVA，依据身份漂移程度缩放参考影响）和锚对比引导（ACG，引导生成远离泛化类别先验）。

Result: 在两分钟以上的生成中，固定锚帧的 DINO-I 从 0.58 降至 0.42，而 Custom Forcing 保持在 0.58–0.62 且不降低运动幅度；主体相似度高于双向定制方法，在 30 秒以上比因果图生视频和参考生视频模型更好地保持身份，且每帧生成速度快 9.5–28.5 倍。

Conclusion: Custom Forcing 无需训练即可为自回归视频模型实现特定主体定制，在长时程身份保持上优于固定锚帧与现有基线，同时生成速度显著更快。

Abstract: Autoregressive video models can generate minute-long videos in real time, but they produce generic subjects from text rather than specific subjects from user-provided images. Existing customization methods either require costly per-subject optimization or use pretrained conditioning networks that jointly process all video frames with bidirectional attention. Neither approach is designed for causal streaming. We present Custom Forcing, a training-free method that stores reference-based anchor frames in the persistent KV cache of a frozen autoregressive video model. However, fixed anchors face two limitations: simple conditioning allows identity to drift, and the text prompt continues to favor a generic subject. To address these problems, drift-adaptive value amplification (DVA) scales reference influence with the degree of identity drift, while anchor contrast guidance (ACG) steers generation away from the generic class prior. Over two-minute rollouts, fixed anchors fall from 0.58 to 0.42 in DINO-I, while Custom Forcing keeps it between 0.58 and 0.62 without reducing motion. Custom Forcing also achieves higher subject similarity than bidirectional customization methods and better preserves identity over 30s than causal image-to-video and reference-to-video models, while generating each frame 9.5--28.5 times faster than these long-video baselines.

</details>


### [35] [Kinematics-Induced Multimodal 3D Human Pose Estimation with Subject-Level Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02943)
*Kaushik Bhargav Sivangi,Fani Deligianni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的多模态3D人体姿态估计框架，融合运动学传感器融合、受试者级隐私审计与私有训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态3D HPE模型基于同一受试者的相关观测训练，存在被记录级分析所忽视的隐私风险。

Method: 1) 对齐模态特定关节表示，注入骨架结构并自适应聚合互补传感器证据；2) 建立黑盒受试者成员推断攻击，辅以逐点最大泄漏分析；3) 通过动作时间分层（人群加权的受试者内采样策略）实现用户级差分隐私。

Result: 在MM-Fi数据集上跨三种不同实验协议进行评测，源代码将于录用后发布。

Conclusion: 该框架将多模态3D HPE与受试者级隐私审计及私有训练统一结合，在保证姿态预测精度的同时量化并缓解个体隐私风险。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D Human Pose Estimation (3D HPE) combines complementary information from RGB, LiDAR, and mmWave radar, but models trained on correlated observations from the same individuals, raise privacy risks overlooked by record level analysis. We present a unified framework for multimodal 3D HPE that couples kinematics-induced sensor fusion with subject level privacy auditing and private training. First, our multimodal model aligns modality specific joint representation, injects skeletal structure and adaptively aggregates complementary sensor evidence for accurate pose prediction. Second, we formulate a black-box subject membership inference attack for 3D HPE, complemented by an empirical pointwise maximal leakage analysis, which characterizes how individual attack score outcomes change inference about the membership outcome.
